### 0. Document authority, risk priority, and closure rule

This section controls how humans and coding agents interpret this execution edition. It does **not** redesign the V3.9 architecture; it prevents stale prose, code-status overclaims, and live-evidence gaps from silently changing the experiment.

**Authority hierarchy:**

1. **Plan §§0–21 are the normative executable design/run contract.**
2. The paired `IMPLEMENTATION_LOG_V3.9-f.8_FINAL_EXECUTION_SAFE.md` is an evidence/status record derived from this Plan. It may record implementation, but it may **not override** the Plan.
3. Historical documents, old runs, and earlier code-status claims carry no current PASS status unless re-established by evidence in the paired current Log.
4. If the Plan and current Log disagree, create `DOC_CONFLICT`, stop the affected path, and correct the Log to the Plan; never improvise a third rule.

**Risk priority used by the Plan and Log:**

| Priority | Meaning | Required resolution point |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **P0 — CRITICAL** | Could invalidate denominators/labels/identity, introduce leakage, violate provider/billing/retention constraints, corrupt raw provenance, or make collection unsafe. | Document rule must be unambiguous before coding; code + offline tests before any affected paid collection; live/provider evidence before Phase 6 when required. |
| **P1 — MAJOR** | Could materially bias sampling/evaluation, confound the window experiment, or make implementation/reproducibility ambiguous. | Resolve before FREEZE; analysis-only P1 settings may be frozen before first model fit where explicitly stated. |
| **P2 — MODERATE** | Traceability/diagnostic/reproducibility weakness that does not by itself redefine the population or target. | Resolve before technical-readiness claim or before affected analysis/publication. |
| **P3 — LOW** | Terminology/history/cosmetic issue with no direct data-validity effect. | Clean before final publication; never allowed to override P0–P2 rules. |

**Three independent status axes:**

```text
DOC_STATUS  = RESOLVED / OPEN
CODE_STATUS = NOT_IMPLEMENTED / CODED / PRODUCTION_WIRED / TESTED
LIVE_STATUS = NOT_REQUIRED / BLOCKED / VERIFIED
```

`DOC_STATUS=RESOLVED` does **not** mean code exists. `CODED` does not mean production-wired. `PRODUCTION_WIRED` does not mean live-verified.

**Document-closure stop condition:** once the active normative Plan and active Log contain no known P0/P1 contradictions under the closure scanner, stop theoretical redesign. Remaining work is implementation, tests, external-reference freeze, authorized live gates, measure→freeze values, and execution evidence. No V3.10.

**Pre-probe freeze vs final manifest:** exogenous traffic/region references, shortlist/replacement list, normalization rules, and probe protocol that must be fixed **before paid Stage 1** are written to a hash-locked `preprobe_reference_freeze_record`. The Phase-5 final manifest later embeds that record unchanged plus all Gate-measured values. This avoids pretending the final manifest already exists before Gate 2.

## 2. Locked architecture (end-to-end)

```text
AeroDataBox webhook (maxDeliveryRetries = 0)       AeroDataBox airport-FIDS REST
        │  1 credit/item, deducted on SEND                │ API-unit ledger
        ▼                                                  ▼
raw_delivery → raw_delivery_item                     raw FIDS response/version + hash
        │                                                  │
        ▼                                                  ▼
flight_events (append-only semantic observations)     flight_population (S1 provider-observable denominator)
        │  ├── every source clock/location fact preserved       │
        │  ├── NEVER reduced to "latest location" first         │
        │  └──► current flight_state (dedup convenience)         │
        │                                                        │
        └── (location observations) ─► raw_airborne_events       │
                                      ─► clean_airborne_points   │
                                      ─► flight_trajectory (S5)  │
                                                               ▼
                                           cutoff-safe snapshot construction
        │
        ▼
cutoff-safe flight_snapshots  (T-24h / T-6h / T-90m; features ≤ cutoff;
        │                       EXISTENCE = population-defined; post-cutoff events supply the LABEL only)
        ▼
flight_airborne_snapshots  (prediction_state = AIRBORNE; one row per (flight, observation_t);  S5)
        ▼
flight_outcomes   (two independent dimensions — NOT mutually exclusive as a single five-state):
        │   flight_operational_state: scheduled / departed / arrived / canceled / canceled_uncertain / diverted / unknown
        │   label_status (per target): pending / observed / censored / missing / not_applicable
        │
        ▼
ML dataset + evaluation  (Month-1 PRE: Engines A/B/C/D + R/P; Engine E deferred; AIRBORNE: Model POST; collection-mechanism ablation)
```

**Two prediction states (see §6.1), never merged into one row:**

```text
PRE-DEPARTURE        cutoff = selected-T horizon (T-24h / T-6h / T-90m;
                     selected T remains BLOCKED until Gate 0.5 verifies its provider-native milestone)
                     predict gate-out delay (departure) / arrival disruption / cancellation / diversion

AIRBORNE (POST)      physical state time = provider location/state observation time
                     decision cutoff = deployable as-of time (`prediction_cutoff_utc`), normally
                     the observation item's `available_at` after durable ingestion
                     valid only under independently verified provider-native airborne movement evidence
                     predict landing ETA, remaining time from the decision cutoff, wheels-on/gate-in delay,
                     P(selected_primary_delay>15), P(selected_primary_delay>60), diversion risk
```

**Three promises the whole plan enforces:**

1. **Never destructively overwrite research provenance** (raw event log +
   current state both kept; dedup is an operational convenience, never the only
   dataset).
2. **No future information in dataset construction or features.** Every source
   fact and frozen deterministic transform must satisfy the §6.1 deployable
   information-availability rule; a semantic event time alone is insufficient.
   Snapshot existence is population-defined, never event-defined.
3. **No foreign/non-experimental billable subscription may contaminate the authorized experimental set** (R1). A canary/probe uses one intended experimental subscription at a time; an authorized Phase-6 batch may contain its intended multiple airport subscriptions, all tied to that batch. R1 makes settled balance deltas attributable to the authorized experimental set; it does **not** imply that every charged provider SEND was received locally.

## 3. Budget & accounting (two budgets — Gate 0)

### 3.1 Two separate pools, one shared quota (the Gate-0 fix)

AeroDataBox exposes **two billable use paths** relevant here—REST/API-unit
requests and Flight-Alert-credit notifications. Their account relationship,
limits, and refill behavior depend on the verified channel and plan; they are
never assumed from a quota size. The 60,000-unit entitlement is user-confirmed;
the subscription channel and current account mechanics are Gate-0 evidence:

- **API quota** (units) — monthly allowance on the marketplace/subscribed plan, spent by REST calls. Under the currently pinned public provider contract, the airport FIDS endpoint is **TIER 2** and the current public pricing maps Tier 2 to **2 API units/request**. Costs for every other endpoint, variable-rate operation, retry, or account-specific plan are **not inherited from legacy estimates**: Gate 0 must freeze the current provider/account evidence and the seven-category REST ledger before billable use. Managed by the verified marketplace/provider subscription terms; entitlement renews according to the verified billing cycle.

**Billing-cycle and budget-day alignment:** The project safety cap is **1,900 Alert credits per Phase-6 `budget_day_id`**, where `budget_day_id=run_day_index` for one parent batch-day. It is not a provider calendar-day rule or a billing-cycle reset. Every SEND is attributed to the immutable owning subscription/batch even if delivery/settlement occurs after UTC midnight; store provider send/attempt date separately. The next batch-day cannot start until the prior budget day is closed and settled under §11.1, with its owned subscriptions deleted or confirmed inactive. Do not delete the budget-day ledger itself: it is an audit record. Billing-cycle boundaries affect only monthly API-unit entitlement. `MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS=60,000` is user-confirmed; Gate 0 verifies the active plan/channel, cycle dates, remaining/used units, and account limits. A renewal never expands the frozen Phase-6 Alert ceiling without re-FREEZE.

`subscription_channel` is a required frozen identity, not a label inferred from
the API hostname or quota size. Gate 0 records the exact channel (for example
`RapidAPI`, `API.Market`, `direct`, or a named custom agreement), account/plan
identifier, cycle, and terms. API units, direct non-expiring API credits, and
Flight-Alert credits are separate resources; no balance or rate limit may be
borrowed across channels or ledgers. If the channel or plan terms cannot be
identified, all billable paths remain BLOCKED.

The `cycle_entitlement_units (=60,000)` line in the API tree applies only when
Gate 0 confirms that the actual subscribed channel exposes the user-confirmed
60,000-unit monthly entitlement. If the verified channel uses a different
quota, a non-expiring direct allocation, or another contract, record that
channel-specific entitlement and its units/expiry rules explicitly; do not
silently substitute 60,000 or mix it with the Alert ledger. Until that mapping
is confirmed and reconciled, the API tree and every billable path remain
BLOCKED.

**Run start date freeze:** The run start date MUST be frozen at FREEZE (before Phase 6 begins). Record `phase6_start_date` in manifest. Once frozen, the start date CANNOT be changed without re-running the entire FREEZE process. The start date determines: (a) billing cycle alignment, (b) weather archive coverage, (c) historical store bootstrap period, (d) evaluation cutoff dates. If the start date is delayed (e.g., Gate 0.5 fails), the FREEZE must be re-run with the new start date.
- **Flight-Alert credit balance** (credits) — dedicated balance, does not
  expire, pauses at 0; spent only by webhook deliveries: **1 credit per flight
  item per delivery attempt, deducted on SEND (not delivery), each retry costs
  another credit.** Refilled via `POST /subscriptions/balance/refill` at
  **1 credit = 1 API unit**. Per-refill and balance-cap limits depend on the
  pricing plan — verify at Gate 0.

### 3.2 Exact Alert/API balance trees (Gate 0)

```text
ALERT BALANCE (credits):
opening_nonexpiring_alert_balance + authorized_alert_refill_credits
  = pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling
  + phase6_alert_spend_ceiling
  + protected_alert_floor
  + ending_alert_margin
  + unallocated_alert_credits

API ENTITLEMENT (units, separately for each billing cycle):
cycle_entitlement_units (=60,000 while the USER-CONFIRMED plan remains active)
  = api_units_consumed_before_freeze
  + authorized_alert_refill_units
  + FIDS_BASE_UNITS + FIDS_SPLIT_UNITS + FIDS_RETRY_UNIT_BUDGET
  + VALIDATION_UNIT_BUDGET + OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET
  + HISTORY_BOOTSTRAP_UNIT_BUDGET + DIAGNOSTIC_UNIT_BUDGET
  + protected_api_floor_units + ending_api_margin_units
  + unallocated_api_units
```

`protected_rest_budget = FIDS_BASE_UNITS + FIDS_SPLIT_UNITS +
FIDS_RETRY_UNIT_BUDGET + VALIDATION_UNIT_BUDGET + OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET +
HISTORY_BOOTSTRAP_UNIT_BUDGET + DIAGNOSTIC_UNIT_BUDGET`.

`ending_alert_margin` is a nonnegative, project-chosen balance reserve in
addition to `protected_alert_floor`; it is frozen at Gate 0 and may not be
silently set to zero merely because it has not yet been measured. Likewise,
`protected_rest_budget` is the frozen API-unit ceiling for the seven REST
categories in §5.4, computed from verified cycle units remaining after
pre-freeze usage and authorized Alert refills, while preserving
`protected_api_floor_units` and `ending_api_margin_units`. Those two API-unit
reserves are nonnegative, project-chosen, and frozen at Gate 0. They are not an
Alert-credit balance and cannot be increased by borrowing from the Alert
ledger. The unsettled-burst reserve has two explicit lifecycle values:
`pre_smoke_unsettled_burst_margin_credits` is a conservative nonnegative
admission reserve frozen at Gate 0 from current account/provider evidence and
is required to admit the safety smoke; `unsettled_burst_margin_credits` is the
measured value frozen after that smoke and carried into later probes/canaries.
The measured value may stay the same or increase, never silently decrease. If
the pre-smoke reserve or any other ledger margin/reserve is unknown, the
corresponding billable path is BLOCKED.

**Rules:**
- `protected_alert_floor = 1,000 Alert credits` is a **project-chosen protected minimum**, not provider law and not a spending target. Gate 0 must prove the balance tree can preserve this floor under the active account. It may be increased before FREEZE for safety; it may not be reduced below 1,000 without an explicit documented design amendment and complete re-FREEZE.
- A prior note mentioned an approximately 2,900-credit balance; that value is
  historical/unverified and **must not be used for admission or budgeting**.
  Gate 0 must record the settled opening non-expiring balance. Pre-run Alert
  work is funded from that opening balance up to its frozen ceiling. If it
  cannot be funded, reduce the Phase-6 ceiling or refill allocation before
  FREEZE; never double-count the same refill credits.
- `pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling` is generated before the first paid action as the exact sum of separately authorized ceilings for: safety smoke; every frozen Stage-1 candidate and replacement; every possible Stage-2 confirmation/replacement under the finite list; Gate 3; Gate 0.5; and the small Gate-4 live check. It is not a forecast of expected use and cannot contain an unbounded "retry/extra probe" bucket. Each run debits its own sub-cap and the aggregate pre-run cap; unused sub-cap is not transferable without a new authorization and updated record.
- `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling` is the maximum experimental ceiling, not a spending target. The **MAX_DESIGN_CEILING is 57,900 credits** as a frozen project-authorized upper bound; it is **not** derived by dividing/subtracting the 60,000-unit monthly entitlement or by assuming all `31×1,900=58,900` daily capacity is spendable. Gate 0 computes and freezes:
  `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling = min(MAX_DESIGN_CEILING, opening_nonexpiring_alert_balance + authorized_alert_refill_credits - pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling - protected_alert_floor - ending_alert_margin)`.
  All terms must be verified, nonnegative, and reconciled. Any remaining authorized balance is `unallocated_alert_credits`; it is **not** automatically spendable by Phase 6. A negative remainder, unexplained balance difference, or failed identity is Gate-0 FAIL/NO-GO.
- FIDS, Gate-5 FIDS, validation, outcome, history, and diagnostic calls consume REST/API units, never Alert credits.
- Track BOTH the active marketplace/provider account usage page (API units) and the Flight-Alert balance (credits); record `subscription_channel` at Gate 0.
- The 1,900 cap is the **per-budget-day ceiling**; `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling≤57,900` is the run-total ceiling. Billing on SEND means a local row count or SOFT_STOP cannot make overshoot impossible. Before every start/continuation, require the owning budget day's settled spend + reservations + the applicable frozen unsettled-burst reserve to fit both budget-day and run remainders. No next budget day starts while a prior one is unsettled. Any settled spend above either ceiling is `MISMATCH`, pauses starts, and requires reconciliation.
- No manual REST-heavy UI actions (Rescore / Simulate / "Rescore all") during the run; all billable REST activity must enter the categorized API-unit ledger.
- **Gate 0:** verify (a) the USER-CONFIRMED 60,000-unit plan is still the active subscribed plan, record `subscription_channel`, and record cycle dates/current remaining units/units already consumed, (b) refill conversion 1 unit = 1 credit and current refill/balance limits, and (c) every billable REST category fits the per-cycle ledger — all before any billable FIDS call. The entitlement constant may be user-confirmed while cycle state remains live evidence.

### 3.3 Three cap concepts (never conflated)

1. **Estimated reservation (before):**
   `budget_day_remaining = 1900 − max(settled_provider_balance_delta_for_budget_day,
   received_delivery_cost_ledger_for_budget_day)` minus the applicable frozen
   unsettled-burst reserve (`pre_smoke_unsettled_burst_margin_credits` before
   the smoke has measured it, otherwise `unsettled_burst_margin_credits`), with active reservations also subtracted
   before admission. The deltas are nonnegative SEND-attributable spend
   quantities; a refill or unrelated balance movement is not silently treated
   as spend. This is an estimate of what a start is allowed to spend, not a
   record of final spend, and it is keyed by immutable `budget_day_id`, not a
   UTC calendar-day label.
2. **In-run safety signal (not final spend):** the watchdog combines settled provider-balance deltas, the received item/attempt-cost ledger, active reservations, and the applicable frozen unsettled-burst reserve for the owning `budget_day_id`. `SOFT_STOP=1900−ADB_DAILY_SOFT_STOP_MARGIN` (50 only as the pre-measure proposal); `HARD_CAP=1900`. Exact spend is known after settlement. Any settled overshoot is `MISMATCH` and blocks starts.
3. **Post-batch reconciliation (after):** settled `C_external = balance_before − balance_stable` is compared with internal item/attempt ledgers. The **isolated Gate-3 canary requires exact equality (`tol=0`)**. Any later production reconciliation tolerance is a separate `PRODUCTION_RECONCILE_TOLERANCE` that must be measured/frozen; until then, nonzero discrepancy is `MISMATCH`. Reconciliation is meaningful only on the exclusive experimental subscription set (R1).

### 3.4 Intended enforcement status

- `adb_collection_batches`: `balance_before/after`, `credits_consumed_actual`,
  `credits_consumed_internal`, `notification_items_received`, rows
  stored/inserted/updated, `delivery_failures`, `reconciliation_status`.
- `adb_ingest_events`: one immutable row per webhook delivery
  (subscription_id, batch_id, received_at, items, stored/inserted/updated/
  skipped, credits_remaining, delivery_failure, error).
- Repository implementation status is **NOT VERIFIED IN THIS DOCUMENT AUDIT**. SEND-aware production enforcement must combine settled provider balance, received delivery cost, internal raw ledger, active reservations, and `unsettled_burst_margin_credits`. Received items or stored rows alone are unsafe because an undelivered SEND can still be charged.
